Carried: OQ-PF-29 (the Filter re-checks its provisional contracts on this GDD landing); OQ-SN-11 (the at-least-3-eligible-surfaces floor, advisory, IW-29); OQ-SN-12 (the INSPECT `check_kind`, reserved for a future channelled verb, IW-25); IA OQ 6 (scanner presentation, HUD's); OQ-PF-4 and OQ-PF-6 (gleam at `k` 1 and under Tungsten and Chemlight, IW-16).

## Acceptance Criteria

*All criteria are GIVEN / WHEN / THEN and ids are `AC-IW-n` (the numbering was kept stable through the QA review, so the groups below are by topic, not contiguous). **Tags.** (auto) an automated test, logic in `tests/unit/instruments/`, integration in `tests/integration/instruments/`; (lint) a build or content lint (instrument data, scene tree, sheets, scripts); (review) a design or lead review of a document or a retained screenshot; (manual) a playtest or a retained screenshot in `production/qa/evidence/`; (spike) a time-boxed engine test whose result may change the design. `(provisional — X)` marks a criterion that depends on a neighbour still being written or a reply owed. **Test-evidence gate.** This is a UI plus Visual / Feel system: every criterion that cites a retained screenshot (AC-IW-5, 9, 25, 29, 48, 100 and the screenshot legs of 1 and 6) is BLOCKING at every `qa.level` including `minimal` (coding standards: a parse check is not a run); Logic criteria (D.1 to D.6, D.8 to D.12, IW-5, IW-11 to IW-14, IW-17, IW-19, IW-21, IW-24) need files in `tests/unit/instruments/`; the card joins with the Filter, Interaction and the Controller are Integration. **No criterion asserts that the indicative IW-29 manifest passes floor (i)** (advisor ruling R17). **Conventions fixed for every vector:** `round` is half away from zero, a "rolling second" is [t, t + 1000 ms), and a **tip-pixel flip** is a frame whose rounded tip coordinate differs from the previous frame's (Formulas intro, D.3, VA-6).*

**Conventions for the criteria below** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*. (1) **Latency oracle.** One convention for AC-IW-33, AC-IW-91 and AC-IW-114: a latency is counted in render frames at a fixed 60 Hz test clock between the frame in which the Adapter receives the cause and the first frame whose draw list reflects it. "Closed in the same render frame" means the instrument's probe `is_card_open()` reads false (and the card root's `visible` is false) by the end of that frame's `_process`; Godot's `queue_free()` runs at the end of the frame, so the test asserts the probe in the same frame and `is_instance_valid(card)` false at the start of the next frame, and never asserts node validity inside the frame of the cause. "Opens on the next render frame" means `is_card_open()` reads true at the end of the frame after the press. (2) **Seeds.** Every randomised test takes its seeds from a fixed list in the test data and prints them; no wall clock, no unseeded RNG. (3) **Machine.** A performance criterion runs on the CI runner and records the runner description in its output; the reference machine (X14) stays open and no machine, build type or spec is named in this document (delegated ruling R11). (4) **Playtest coding rule.** For every (manual) playtest criterion the protocol is a scripted session, the observed statement is the tester's first unprompted explanation, it is coded by two coders against the criterion's word list (or the codes named in the criterion), a disagreement is settled by the producer, and the raw codes are retained in `production/qa/evidence/`.

### A. Ownership, boundaries and the forbidden list

- **AC-IW-1** (lint, plus review) [IW-1, IW-28, R16, UI-3]: (lint) GIVEN the built level scene tree and scripts, WHEN the lint runs, THEN (a) only scripts under `instruments/` and `inspect_card/` call `perceived()` for a GRAPH_* ref or touch `read_view`; (b) no node class or node name from the deny-list exists: schematic, ship_overlay, status_overlay, minimap, ship_status, loop_summary, sanity_meter, lie_counter, stage_name, spotted_stat, attention_indicator, threat_indicator, condition_word, status_bar, condition_bar, rating, loot_value, weld_preview, alarm_word (matched as whole tokens; the mandated nodes `wear_overlay`, `patch_plate`, `dead_frame`, `housing_pip` and `alarm_mark` are on an allow-list and are not matches) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*; (c) no identifier `surge` is read by presentation code (the weld `surge[]` is never printed); (d) no instrument script references Attention, the Sanity ledger read APIs, a crewmate or a horror entity. (review, retained screenshot sweep in `production/qa/evidence/`) GIVEN a captured tour of the home ship, derelict A and derelict B, WHEN the tester walks the UI-3 checklist, THEN no screen shows a sanity meter, lie counter, stage name, "spotted" statistic, Attention or threat indicator, condition word, bar, rating, loot value, weld-preview number or Alarm Red word. (A string scan cannot find "a bar" or "a rating", hence the review leg.)
- **AC-IW-2** (lint, plus auto integration) [IW-2]: (lint) GIVEN the registries after boot, WHEN enumerated, THEN Instruments register zero hooks, mutators, state blocks, dirty keys, command types and persisted fields. (auto, integration) GIVEN a save, WHEN restored with and without the Instruments module loaded, THEN `state_hash` (KRN D.12) is bit-identical.
- **AC-IW-3** (lint) [IW-5, R18, PF-3]: GIVEN all instrument and card code, WHEN grepped and scene-linted, THEN there is no call to `true_value`, `summary()`, `power_use()`, `can_join()` or `block_hash()`; no `Perceived` is held across frames (only the scalars of IW-11); no address by `peer_id`.
- **AC-IW-4** (auto) [IW-3, E-IW-17]: GIVEN `lit(ref)` oscillating around `LIT_ON_PM` 150 / `LIT_OFF_PM` 120 for 600 frames with the card open, WHEN the frames are drawn, THEN the card closes exactly once and does not reopen without a new press, and the face's visibility is never changed by instrument code (only by the renderer).
- **AC-IW-5** (manual, retained screenshots, with a measured leg) [IW-3] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*: GIVEN an unlit gauge, the same gauge under the chest beam, under a fixture and under a thrown Chemlight glowstick (`#86B896`), WHEN captured at `k` 3, THEN the needle-to-face luminance contrast ratio (WCAG relative luminance of the needle pixels over the face pixels in the capture) is below 1.3 when unlit and at least 3.0 under each light, the three zone-band codes are distinguishable under each light by the AC-IW-24 metric, and no caption contains "hand lamp" (grep). The ratios 1.3 and 3.0 are `[PLACEHOLDER]`s set at S-A1.
- **AC-IW-6** (auto, plus measured) [IW-4, R21] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*: (auto) GIVEN a loop with `served_pm` above 0, WHEN its gauge is in the dark, THEN a housing pip exists on the emissive layer (mask bit 2), casts no light and does not set `lit(ref)`; with `served_pm` 0 the pip is absent; under a stage-2 HOUSING_GLOW lie the pip may be shown or hidden against truth without changing `lit(ref)`. (measured, retained screenshot) GIVEN a running loop's gauge in the dark, THEN the pip's luminance exceeds the surrounding plate by at least 18 L* (VA-2.4) and the face region of the pipped capture differs from the unpipped capture in at most 2 % of pixels (a pixel differs when any channel differs by more than 4 of 255).
- **AC-IW-62** (auto) [IW-5, E-IW-40, E-IW-48]: GIVEN 20 on-screen instruments, 1 off-screen and 1 in an unloaded section, with one card open, WHEN 120 frames are drawn, THEN a Filter stub counts exactly one `perceived(ref, local PlayerId)` per on-screen ref per frame (the open card adds none), zero for the off-screen and unloaded refs, and the non-value `read_view` reads (tremor predicate, frame selection, WORN, alarm, binding validity) happen once per `GRAPH_STEP_TICKS` 6 per instrument (20 reads per instrument per 120 ticks, 400 for 20 instruments; 120 frames equal 120 ticks at the fixed 60 Hz test clock) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*, never per frame; the first pull after a cull snaps with no ramp or settle.
- **AC-IW-86** (lint) [KRN H10, IW-31]: GIVEN the sim-layer scripts, WHEN scanned, THEN none holds a reference to an instrument node or calls Instruments; a kernel run with the Instruments scenes absent produces the same `state_hash`.

### B. The instrument family and its data

- **AC-IW-7** (lint) [IW-6]: GIVEN every instrument definition, WHEN rendered, THEN one data definition drives both the wall node and the card; no world gauge carries a digit, a numeral glyph or any text (sprite and string scan); no READOUT kind exists in the world layer.
- **AC-IW-8** (lint) [IW-7, D.2]: GIVEN the DIAL_L and DIAL_S sheets, WHEN measured, THEN each matches this table: DIAL_L needle 24, band inner r 25, band 3, outer r 28, arc 56 x 42, housing 60 x 46 with a 2-px bezel, cell 96 x 48; DIAL_S needle 18, band inner r 19, band 3, outer r 22, arc 44 x 33, housing 46 x 35 with a 1-px bezel, cell 48 x 48; both `arc_deg` 240, needle stroke 2 art px, hub 4 px; both needles are at least `NEEDLE_LEN_MIN_PX` 16; no authored record has a needle of exactly 16 (a 16 record is accepted only as a Filter-eligibility test fixture).
- **AC-IW-9** (review, with retained screenshot and a forced-choice test) [IW-7, K-7] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*: GIVEN both housings composed on a wall face beside the 16-px crewmate helmet, WHEN the ART 3.1 silhouette test and the helmet-scale test are run, THEN (a) the measured round-feature diameters pass the helmet-scale test (arc outer 56 and 44, hub 4: none in 5.3-32 art px), and (b) in a forced-choice test at 25 % scale with at least 6 raters, each shown the DIAL_L and DIAL_S housing silhouettes mixed with 6 crewmate silhouettes and asked 'wall instrument or crew', at least 90 % of the housing silhouettes are classified as wall instruments; if (a) or (b) fails the octagonal-arc fallback is adopted for both sizes (R-VA-2).
- **AC-IW-10** (lint) [IW-8, PF-45, PF-44]: GIVEN every record in `assets/data/instruments/`, WHEN validated, THEN each has `instrument_id`, `gauge_kind`, `quantity`, `arc_deg` (or `travel_px`), `lsb`, `zones[]` as LS-7 band names, `beyond_witness` and a `lie_surface_id` for each corruptible surface that is present in `lie_targets.json`; no `lie_surface_id` ends in `.readout`; a deliberately malformed record yields the honest path plus `lie_unrenderable` at runtime and a build failure for any kind in `lie_targets.json`.
- **AC-IW-11** (lint) [IW-8, ART:1207]: GIVEN every `mach_*`, `ui_*` and `ovl_*` sheet, WHEN the reserved-hue scan runs, THEN no pixel lies in the violet (270-330 degrees, S above 25 %) or flesh (5-30 degrees, S 20-60 %, V 45-85 %) bands; each lie-capable gauge sheet has the slices `glass`, `needle_edge` and `needle`; both honest frames of every pair sit on one sheet page.
- **AC-IW-12** (lint) [IW-9]: GIVEN the instrument data and the DA placements, WHEN linted, THEN `quantity` is in the closed set, zones are derived from LS-7 band names (no threshold number appears in instrument data), `INSTR_QUANTITY_COLLISION` fails a duplicated `(subject, quantity)` pair (two such gauges would share one lie and not be a second opinion; the former one-instrument-per-room fallback is retired, LS-19 additions), and `T` on a DIAL_S fails the zone lint (D.2) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*.
- **AC-IW-13** (lint, plus auto) [IW-10]: (lint) GIVEN the instrument code, WHEN scanned, THEN there is no `draw_line`, `draw_polyline` or `draw_arc` call for a needle, zone, tick or bezel. (auto) GIVEN 20 sampled angles across -120 to +120 degrees, WHEN the needle is drawn, THEN the tip lies on whole art px (rounding half away from zero) and the stroke is 2 art px wide.
- **AC-IW-67** (auto) [D.2]: GIVEN span 0..300 dkPa, WHEN `pm(v)` runs, THEN pm(0) = 0, pm(1) = 3, pm(150) = 500, pm(300) = 1000, pm(-5) = 0, pm(305) = 1000; zones tile 0..1000 with no gap or overlap and widths summing to 1000; `pm_min` is 57.3 (DIAL_L) and 75.4 (DIAL_S); a 67 pm zone passes on DIAL_L (7.0 px) and fails on DIAL_S (5.3 px); the `load` edge at 1000 permille maps to 800 pm of a 0..1250 span.
- **AC-IW-82** (lint) `(provisional — Layout and Derelict Authoring)` [IW-7, IW-31]: GIVEN the placements, WHEN linted, THEN each record carries `instrument_id` (a valid key in `assets/data/instruments/`), `node_ref` or `room_key` plus `quantity`, `mount` in {WALL_FACE, MACHINE_PANEL} and `host_face`; DIAL_L is never mounted on a pipe run; DIAL_S sits only at a terminal fitting (centred on the band mid-row 43.5) or on a MACHINE_PANEL.
- **AC-IW-88** (lint) [E-IW-51, IW-37] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*: GIVEN each shipped locale, WHEN checked, THEN the numeral cells and the dial or glass render in every locale; every glyph of every `name_key` and `unit_key` exists in the bitmap instrument face, or that one element falls back to the default-locale string and then to the part-number stencil; the card is never removed for a locale.

### C. Honest motion

- **AC-IW-14** (auto) [D.1, IW-11]: GIVEN `prev_honest_pm` 617, `displayed_pm` 600, `T_c - t_sample` 3 and `tick_alpha` 0.5 on DIAL_L, WHEN the needle is drawn, THEN `pm_honest` is 607.1 (plus or minus 0.05) and the tip is (+10, -22); at `a` 1 the draw equals `displayed_pm` and the needle rests.
- **AC-IW-15** (auto) [IW-11, C14]: GIVEN a slow vent that crosses one `p_o2` quantum every 3 s, WHEN 12 s of frames are drawn, THEN the needle ramps only in the 100 ms after each crossing, the tip-pixel flip count over any 1 s window outside a ramp is 0, and the drawn value never leaves [prev, new] (no extrapolation); a first pull after a cull snaps with no ramp and no settle.
- **AC-IW-16** (auto) [D.4]: GIVEN HONEST, `INSTR_SETTLE_ZETA` 0.7, `INSTR_SETTLE_OMEGA` 20 and a jump from 300 pm to 700 pm from rest, WHEN sampled, THEN tau 100 ms draws 590.3 pm (plus or minus 0.5), tau 220 ms draws 718.4 pm (plus or minus 0.5) and tau 400 ms draws exactly 700; for a 240 pm jump the overshoot is at most 5 % (4.6 % at the defaults), the exact 2 % settling time (the last sample with `abs(1 - y)` > 0.02, 0.1 ms resolution) is between 250 and 400 ms (299 ms at the defaults, plus or minus 1 ms; the approximation 286 ms is not asserted) and the value equals the target exactly from 400 ms on *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*; and the joint lint (2) rejects 0.8 / 20 / 400 (exact 188 ms) and accepts 0.7 / 20 / 400.
- **AC-IW-17** (auto) [D.4, IW-11] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*: GIVEN integer `displayed_pm` samples at Graph-step cadence and the defaults (`INSTR_REST_STEPS` 3, `INSTR_SETTLE_WINDOW_STEPS` 4, `INSTR_SETTLE_JUMP_PM` 40), WHEN each run starts from rest (3 steps without a sample), THEN: a single `p_o2` quantum (16 or 17 pm) ramps with no settle; two quanta in one step (33 pm) ramp; a 39 pm accumulated change inside the window ramps; a 40 pm accumulated change inside the window starts a settle (boundary); three `p_o2` quanta (17 + 17 + 16 pm) over 300 ms start a settle at the sample that reaches 40; a 40 pm change that arrives after the window (the 5th step) is a sweep and starts no settle; a run whose previous sample was only 2 steps earlier is not from rest and starts no settle, while 3 steps without a sample re-arm it (with the previous sample at step n, a sample at n + 3 has 2 empty steps and is not from rest, and a sample at n + 4 has 3 empty steps and is from rest; a window opened by the first sample at step w counts samples at w to w + 3 inclusive, and a sample at w + 4 is outside it) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*; a 400 pm jump from rest starts a settle; a sustained ramp of 45 pm per step starts one settle at its first step and each later step re-bases the one triple without starting a second settle.
- **AC-IW-18** (auto) [IW-11 (d)]: GIVEN a settle in flight, WHEN a further honest change re-bases the triple, THEN the drawn value on the re-base frame equals the value on the frame before it (no jump), only one triple exists, and velocity is discarded.

### D. Lying surfaces, state frames and the alarm mark

- **AC-IW-25** (manual, retained screenshots) [IW-16, IW-26]: GIVEN a lying dial with the card open, WHEN captured at `k` 1, 2 and 3 and at `H` 3, THEN the gleam (`#B07BE6` fringe, never the core) is visible on the wall glass and on the card glass and needle edge at 2 screen px or more, and the card frame, bezel and rivets carry no violet pixel.
- **AC-IW-26** (auto) [D.9]: GIVEN `k` 1 to 8, WHEN `gleam_edge_art_px` is computed, THEN the result is 2 at `k` 1 and 1 at every `k` of 2 or more (screen px 2, 2, 3, ... 8); the card edge is `H_card` screen px (3 or more); and the dev counter `diag.gleam_over_cap` stays at 0 for any amplitude up to 942 pm.
- **AC-IW-27** (spike) `(provisional — Perception Filter spike S-1)` [IW-16, OQ-IW-8]: GIVEN the Filter's per-instance parameter path with the card and the needle-edge mask in its matrix, WHEN run with a gleam under Tungsten and Chemlight, THEN the gleam adds zero draw calls, its hue stays inside the violet band under coloured light, and if it fails the recorded cut order (housing and glyph gleams first, never the needle or the card numeral well) is applied; if the per-instance parameter is unavailable (`[UNV]` in 4.7.2) the IW-16 fallback (a child edge sprite with `modulate`) adds zero ShaderMaterials and one draw per lying on-screen instrument *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*.
- **AC-IW-28** (auto, plus review) [IW-17]: (auto) GIVEN node states, WHEN the Presenter selects frames, THEN state 0 with `served_pm` above 0 gives `running`; state 0 with `served_pm` 0 gives `stopped`; state 1 without a hazard mode gives `running` or `stopped` plus the wear overlay; state 1 with a hazard mode gives `running` or `stopped` by `served_pm` (plus wear), `cracked` only for a housing or tank under LEAK or BREACH, and the lamp pair follows `served_pm`; state 2 gives `stopped` (pump, rotor) or `cracked` (housing, tank) with the lamp pair `unlit`; state 3 gives `cracked` plus the DEAD frame; states 4 and 5 draw nothing; a `frame_override` yields the other frame of the same pair only; a pair missing one frame fails `has_both_frames` and the lint; the glyph is an 8x8 lens, run a filled lens with a 2-px inner tick, stop a hollow lens with a 2-px diagonal bar (shape check automated). (review) run and stop are distinguishable in greyscale.
- **AC-IW-29** (review, with retained screenshot) [IW-18]: GIVEN a WORN subject, a patched subject and an unworn one, WHEN captured, THEN wear shows only as the overlay frame and patch plate and the gauge reads the module's output; no word, bar, rating, colour grade or numeral says "worn"; the patch plate persists.
- **AC-IW-30** (auto) [IW-19, SN L-A]: GIVEN a bound subject, WHEN `alarm_eligible` is false then true, THEN the mark is visible exactly while it is true; it is not drawn at `sev` 3 or more while the fairness gate is closed; it is never the first warning on an endogenous transition (the 10 s `W_MIN_WARNING_S` fixture shows a prior tell); it is unchanged by any HOUSING_GLOW or MACHINE_FRAME lie stub; it is absent on a DIAL_S (E-IW-14) and on the card; it pulses with a period of 2 to 3 s and never above 3 Hz and holds solid under reduced motion.
- **AC-IW-79** (lint, plus review) [IW-19, VA-2.6]: GIVEN the alarm mark asset, WHEN inspected, THEN it is 16x16, Alarm Red `#E0102A`, world exclamation stroke Void Steel (HUD twin Paper White), non-emissive, casts no light, lit-gated by the renderer, sits on the housing and not the face, exists on DIAL_L housings, panels and tanks only, and no code path reads `sev` to decide it; (review) the fallback "no mark" is a one-flag change.
- **AC-IW-107** (lint, plus auto) [IW-19, IW-29 (v), E-IW-37, Beat C] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*: (lint) GIVEN the instrument data and the placements, WHEN linted, THEN `ALARM_MARK_ON_REMOTE` (`hops(mount_room, subject_room)` >= 1 AND a mark-bearing housing) fails the build, and every mark is bound to the machine or console panel, tank prop or compartment its housing physically belongs to (IW-19 rows (a), (b), (c), (f)); (auto) one case per table row (a)-(h) asserts mark yes or no as tabulated, all 18 manifest rows evaluate to the 'IW-19 row' column of the IW-29 table (6 mark-capable, 12 not), and the mark's presence or absence is never an input to `motion_mode`.
- **AC-IW-109** (auto, integration) [Beat C, IW-29 (vi), IW-8] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review; BLOCKING 1)* (run twice, once per `seat_scanner_usable` input of the test-owned fixture manifest; with the input false the fixtures are reported scanner-dependent and `NO_BEAT_C_FIXTURE` is not raised for a fixture that has a FIXED partner): GIVEN fixture B (`inst_b_dock_p_o2`, `mount_room` `b_spine`, `subject_room` `b_dock`, tree `b_dock - b_spine`), WHEN `hops` is computed over `neighbors(cid)`, THEN it is 1 (at most `WITNESS_HOPS_MAX` 2), the computed flags are REMOTE, the seat in `b_spine` is not in the subject room (L-G allows the FALSE_OK), and with a FALSE_OK stub on the ref and an `alarm_eligible` descriptor on the seam leak in `b_dock` the housing draws a NORMAL-zone needle, the gleam and the motion mode and **no** Alarm Red mark, while a machine panel mounted in `b_dock` on the alarm-eligible node does show its mark; fixture A (`inst_a_machine_T`, `a_spine` -> `a_machine`, `hops` 1 over `a_dock - a_spine - {a_life, a_machine}`) passes the same assertions with a cold-band descriptor (a stub: whether Sanity makes the A lie eligible is OQ-IW-35).

- **AC-IW-78** (auto) [IW-16, D.9]: GIVEN a lying gauge, WHEN the pixels are sampled, THEN every gleam pixel lies on the `glass`, `needle_edge` or housing-edge slice (card: glass, needle edge, digit-well top and bottom edge), the colour is `#B07BE6`, never `#8A3FD1`, never a fill; the drawn alpha equals `min(leak_amplitude_pm / 1000, 0.942)` (D.9); `diag.gleam_over_cap` is 0 at 942 pm and rises at 943 pm (0.943 x 0.902 = 0.8506 above 0.85), where the draw alpha is clamped to 0.942; at 1000 pm the drawn alpha is 0.942 and the peak channel is 0.850 *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*.

- **AC-IW-81** (auto, with an audio-request recorder) [IW-32, AAV-17] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*: GIVEN a `p_o2` falling over 20 s and a store draining over 20 s, WHEN sampled in 1 s windows, THEN in at least 95 % of the windows in which the needle or meniscus value changed (a detent needle rests between quanta while the hum varies continuously, so a window with no needle change is excluded from the denominator; `sign` is three-valued and a zero change on one side with a non-zero change on the other counts as a mismatch only inside a counted window) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)* the sign of the change of the hum pitch parameter requested by Audio equals the sign of the change of the needle or meniscus value, of the machine frame state (`running` versus `stopped`) and of the lamp brightness (`served_pm`); a window in which the hum falls while the rotor frame is `running` at full cadence occurs only under a SPRITE_SWAP lie stub.

### E. The inspect view

- **AC-IW-32** (auto) `(provisional — Interaction reply owed)` [IW-21, IA-38, AC-IA-112]: GIVEN two instruments in reach, WHEN `inspect` is pressed, THEN it acts only on the focused lit INSTRUMENT; `interact` on an instrument does nothing; no command type named `inspect` exists in the kernel command registry; the verb key is state-neutral.
- **AC-IW-33** (auto) [IW-24, E-IW-15 to E-IW-26, E-IW-52]: one test case per condition, each asserting `is_card_open()` is false by the end of the same render frame (latency oracle above) and the card node is freed by the start of the next: `lit(ref)` false, `cancel` (X), B, a second `inspect`, kernel restore, `hard_start`, binding lost, reach lost, section transit, movement past the dead-zone, a context change (`chart_table` including pause, `chat`) and a `loco_state` change to STAGGERED, DOWNED, DEAD or IN_POD, and that none of them re-opens the card on return *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*. GIVEN a press at tick 4 after a lit focus lands, or at tick 4 after a close, THEN the card opens on the next render frame (the debounce delays the plate, never the press) and requests the open sound once; no cooldown follows a deliberate close; a card never opens from `chart_table` or `chat`; and the card appears on the next render frame as a cut (transform and modulate are constant across the opening frame: no scale, fade or slide).
- **AC-IW-34** (auto) `(provisional — PF-7, PF-9, PF-18 re-check on this GDD landing, OQ-PF-29)` [IW-26, AC-PF-23]: GIVEN a Filter stub returning each `motion_mode` and each `lie_kind` for one ref, WHEN the wall node and the card are drawn in the same frame, THEN needle angle, gleam amplitude and phase, motion mode and digits are equal on both; a lie starting, ending or changing while the card is open appears on both in the same frame with no flash of the true value; closing and reopening on an active lie shows the same `Perceived` and requests no new sound.
- **AC-IW-35** (auto) [IW-25, R13]: GIVEN a Sanity ledger stub, WHEN the card is opened and closed, THEN `record_falsification` is never called, no command is submitted and nothing is replicated.
- **AC-IW-36** (auto) [IW-22, K-3]: GIVEN every card variant, WHEN its node tree and strings are scanned, THEN it contains exactly a label (`name_key`), the needle or meniscus with zone bands, and one numeral with its unit; no OK, FAILED, DEGRADED, WORN, LOW, CRITICAL, reason string, true value beside a lying one, alarm mark, loop glyph, wear frame, lie count or stage name; every string is a `tr()` StringName key.
- **AC-IW-37** (auto) [D.6, IW-23]: GIVEN the five displays at default and maximum `H`, WHEN `card_layout` runs, THEN `H_card` and the plate size match the D.7 card table (720p `k_auto` 2: `H` 3 gives 216 px and `H` 6 gives `H_card` 5, 360 px; 1280x800 `k_auto` 2: `H` 6 gives 5, 360 px; 1080p `k_auto` 3: `H` 3 gives 216 px, `H` 6 gives 432 px; 1440p `k_auto` 4: `H` 4 gives 288 px, `H` 8 gives 576 px; 2160p `k_auto` 6: `H` 6 gives 432 px, `H` 12 gives 864 px); over 200 seeded randomised chest-point and instrument layouts, whenever at least one candidate satisfies (a) to (e) the chosen plate satisfies all five, otherwise `H_card` is stepped down toward `HUD_MIN_SCALE` and the candidates retried, and only then is a soft overlap accepted in the IW-23 violation order (the retired "farthest from the chest point" fallback is not tested) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*; the plate position is on the HUD art-px grid (`pos = H_card x round(pos / H_card)`); the keep-out radii scale with `k` (28 x `k`, 12 x `k`); the plate follows `world_to_hud` per frame under drift, and the side is re-chosen only on a cut (zoom, resize) or when the latched rect fails (a), (b) or (c), and that re-choice is a cut, never a slide. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* LINEAR vectors: a LINEAR card has `plate_h` 88 (95 with a wrapped label); 1080p `H` 6 gives `H_card` 6 and a 432 x 528 px plate; 720p `H` 6 gives `H_card` 4 and 288 x 352 px; 1440p `H` 8 gives 8 and 576 x 704 px; 2160p `H` 12 gives 12 and 864 x 1056 px. Fallback vectors: the D.6 fixture (720p, `H` 6, `k` 4) yields `H_card` 3 with the right candidate slid up to y 36..252, never over the instrument, the chest disc or the beam corridor; a candidate whose clamped rectangle overlaps the instrument is never chosen; a teammate disc, a lit interactable footprint or a `witness_anchor_px` disc inside a candidate counts as a violation (AC-IW-115).
- **AC-IW-38** (auto) [IW-37, E-IW-28]: GIVEN a label wider than the plate's text width, WHEN the card opens, THEN it wraps to a second row, `plate_h` becomes 79, `H_card` is recomputed against the taller plate (at 720p and `H` 6 it falls from 5 to 4 and the plate is 288 x 316 px), and the label is never truncated.
- **AC-IW-39** (auto) `(ratified 2026-10-27 — user ruling D94; no longer provisional)` [IW-25, E-IW-29] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*: GIVEN the `inspect_card` context, WHEN the context is entered, THEN `set_move_intent {NONE}` is sent, the run latch is cleared and a running channel continues (PC contexts note); WHEN a movement input past `MOVE_DEADZONE` arrives, THEN the card closes and the first `set_move_intent` with that octant is submitted in the same frame (no press is swallowed), while a stick inside the dead-zone does nothing; GIVEN a direction key held when `inspect` is pressed, THEN the card stays open and the avatar does not move until that key is released and pressed again (or the octant changes) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*; GIVEN a gamepad stick at 0.05 beyond `MOVE_DEADZONE` (sub-margin noise) for 120 frames THEN the card stays open, and at `MOVE_DEADZONE + CARD_PAD_DEADZONE_MARGIN` + 0.01 it closes and the avatar moves in the same frame; zoom works; `inspect`, `cancel` (X) and B close the card; `set_aim` stays live and the cursor stays hidden and inert (PC-16, HS-52, closed).
- **AC-IW-40** (auto) [IW-33, E-IW-25]: GIVEN a card open and a settle in flight *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review, delegated ruling G2)*, WHEN the kernel pauses (the context change) and stays paused for 30 s THEN the card is closed in the same render frame (AC-IW-114), and WHEN resumed THEN the drawn value is bit-identical before and after, the card is still closed (no plate animates in, no open sound is requested) and no jump occurs; GIVEN a movement direction held through the pause and resume, THEN the card does not re-open, no `inspect` is synthesised, and the Instruments layer consumes no movement input; after `hard_start` or a restore the card is closed, every presentation scalar is reset and the needle shows `displayed_pm` from the first frame.
- **AC-IW-71** (auto) [IW-21, IW-3, E-IW-18]: GIVEN a lit focused instrument for at least `PROMPT_SHOW_DEBOUNCE_TICKS` ticks (13, HUD-owned; read by name from the HUD constant, never hard-coded in the test) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*, THEN the prompt shows the state-neutral `verb_inspect` text ("R . Inspect"; RT on gamepad); it is hidden in the same frame `lit(ref)` falls; an unlit instrument is not focusable; the prompt contains no state word.
- **AC-IW-72** (auto) [E-IW-19, E-IW-22, E-IW-23, E-IW-24]: GIVEN a card open, WHEN focus moves to another instrument, THEN the card stays on its latched ref; WHEN a teammate's beam keeps `lit(ref)` true and the local player leaves reach, THEN the card closes; WHEN `lit` turns on during `chart_table` or `chat`, THEN no card opens; WHEN `inspect` is pressed on a focused ref with no record, THEN the press is consumed silently, `diag.inspect_unresolved` rises by 1 and no text or sound appears.
- **AC-IW-73** (auto, integration) [E-IW-30]: GIVEN a card open, WHEN a breach starts, THEN the kernel tick counter keeps advancing, the HUD critical strip updates and `inspect` closes the card in one press at no other cost.
- **AC-IW-74** (auto) [E-IW-26, IW-23]: GIVEN a card open, WHEN the window is resized or `k` or `H` changes, THEN every placement is re-projected, the card stays open, no settle fires, the numeral scheduler timestamp is reset, and a side change is a cut; the plate is opaque, on the HUD grid, and nothing behind it is dimmed or blurred.
- **AC-IW-75** (auto) `(default applied 2026-10-27, pending user confirmation — D96)` [IW-12, K-18] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*: GIVEN a stressed HONEST gauge with the card open and `reduced_motion` off, THEN the card needle trembles by at most plus or minus 1 HUD art px (not multiplied by `H_card`) and the frame, plate and keycaps never move; GIVEN the same state with `reduced_motion` on, THEN the card needle does not tremble (offset exactly 0) and the held notch pair (two Hull Grey squares on the Ground plate above the dial) shows while the predicate holds and the reading is HONEST, and neither tremor nor notch ever shows on a lying reading.

- **AC-IW-77** (auto) [IW-22, IW-14, IW-23]: GIVEN the Filter on and off, THEN the card frame pixels are bit-identical and never gleam; the card's honest numeral changes only on the HUD's shared grid boundary and adds no event to `hud_flash_budget` (HS-47); the card is outside the HUD at-rest non-Ground cap measurement.
- **AC-IW-85** (auto, co-op tier) [E-IW-48, E-IW-57, E-IW-58]: GIVEN two seats where one is hallucinating, THEN each seat's wall gauge and card equal its own `Perceived`; both beams lighting one gauge keeps `lit(ref)` true for both; a card is never visible to or replicated to the other seat and a second seat's card is independent of the first; an unresolved `PlayerId` draws the honest path for that frame.
- **AC-IW-113** (auto, keyboard and gamepad) `(provisional — PC-6 and IA-2 amendment, OQ-IW-39)` [IW-21, E-IW-61] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*: GIVEN a valve (or repair target) holding the primary focus and a lit gauge on the same panel in reach and arc, WHEN `interact` is pressed (E, or A on a pad), THEN the valve acts and no card opens; WHEN `inspect` is pressed (R, or RT on a pad), THEN the gauge's card opens from the secondary slot and the valve is not acted on; the plate lists both verbs; with the gauge unlit, `inspect` does nothing and the plate shows the valve verb only.

### F. Content floors, fallbacks and diagnostics

- **AC-IW-41** (lint) [IW-29, R17]: GIVEN the instrument data and Derelict Authoring placements, WHEN the floor lints run, THEN (ii) at least 3 lie-eligible surfaces per site (advisory), (iii) a consequence-channel witness within `WITNESS_HOPS_MAX` 2 sharing no node with the instrument, and (iv) no decorative gauge (computed `beyond_witness` set non-empty, STATE for GLYPH and FRAME) are checked with pass and fail results; floor (i) (`SECOND_INSTRUMENT_MIN` second reading per loop, per seat and per direction, with the scanner a credit only when `seat_scanner_usable`) is reported as findings listing every loop and instrument without a partner *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review; BLOCKING 1)*. **No criterion asserts that the indicative manifest as listed passes floor (i)**; AC-IW-83 carries the fixtures.
- **AC-IW-42** (auto) [IW-30, E-IW-43]: GIVEN a bound node DESTROYED, SALVAGED or ABSENT, WHEN drawn, THEN the DEAD frame (a 1-px Hull Grey diagonal hairline and a 4-px needle stub at a fixed angle) shows with no prompt, card, gleam, tremor or pip and carries no `lie_surface_id`; a compartment-bound gauge is never DEAD while the compartment exists; a ref that cannot be resolved gives DEAD, whereas a missing or malformed calibration record gives the honest path plus `lie_unrenderable`, and the two are not confused.
- **AC-IW-43** (lint) [D.8]: GIVEN the manifest, WHEN `instrument_budget` runs, THEN `N_loaded` is at most `INSTRUMENT_SHARE_MAX` 24 (14 with home plus derelict B, 18 with both derelicts welded) and, with Interaction's lint, `F + L_seed + C` is at most `INTERACT_FIXTURE_BUDGET`.
- **AC-IW-44** (auto) [IW-27, D.10]: GIVEN reduced motion, WHEN a jump and a stress predicate occur, THEN the settle is a step; the notch pair (two Void Steel nicks in the bezel top edge, each at least 4 screen px at every `k`, and two Hull Grey squares on the card above the dial) shows only while the predicate holds and `motion_mode` is HONEST and is removed in the frame a lie starts; the alarm mark holds solid; the Filter's glide, wander and lying tick behave per PF-39 (not asserted here).
- **AC-IW-45** (lint) `(provisional — Adaptive Audio reply owed)` [IW-32]: GIVEN instrument code, WHEN scanned, THEN it requests no sound directly and publishes only the presentation signals `card_opened` and `card_closed`.
- **AC-IW-46** (auto, plus lint) [IW-38]: (auto) GIVEN a dev build, WHEN `drawn_value(ref)` is read, THEN it equals what was drawn; `ref_key` is a stable integer, unique per instrument and identical across a restore; `instrument_diag` returns exactly {`instrument_id`, `ref_key`, `motion_mode` as drawn, `settle_active`, `tremor_active`, `card_open`, `state`}; neither probe returns `true_value`. (lint) a release build contains no `drawn_value` or `instrument_diag` symbol and the Overlay treats an absent probe as `bypass_check: ABSENT`, never clean (DO-22). (auto) GIVEN a dev build at BOOTING, WHEN the Overlay enumerates channels, THEN exactly one Instruments channel exists with the four counters (AC-DO-69); (lint) a release build registers none.
- **AC-IW-47** (auto) [IW-36, D.7]: GIVEN every `k` in [1, `k_auto` + 2] and `H` from `H_default` to 2 x `H_default` on the five displays, WHEN legibility is computed, THEN the needle stroke is at least 2 screen px, the gleam and tremor art-px sizes follow D.5 and D.9, the numeral cap is at least 21 and the label cap at least 15 screen px at `H_card` 3, DIAL_L meets `NEEDLE_SCREEN_MIN_PX` at every rung and DIAL_S from `k` 2; `k_auto` = floor(min(W / 640, H / 360)) gives 2, 2, 3, 4, 6 for 720p, 1280x800, 1080p, 1440p, 2160p; and the locate-only flag (`k / k_auto` below 0.5) is exactly: 720p and 1280x800 `k` 1 (0.5) not locate-only; 1080p `k` 1 (0.33) yes, `k` 2 (0.67) no; 1440p `k` 1 yes, `k` 2 (0.5) no; 2160p `k` 1 and `k` 2 yes, `k` 3 (0.5) no.
- **AC-IW-83** (lint) [IW-29, E-IW-46]: GIVEN a test-local fixture manifest (not the indicative one), THEN floor (i) emits one finding per unpartnered loop instrument (the unpartnered-by-a-fixed-gauge rows in the indicative list are the regression fixture, with `seat_scanner_usable` false: every row marked scanner-dependent in the IW-29 table *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review; BLOCKING 1)*); floor (iv) rejects an instrument whose computed `beyond_witness` set is empty (a local `load` dial); `cryo_bay` with a lie-eligible `p_o2` and a lie-eligible `T` passes `INSTR_QUANTITY_COLLISION` and a duplicated `(cryo_bay, p_o2)` pair fails it *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*; a site with 2 eligible surfaces raises the advisory (ii) warning.

(A flat "AC-IW-14 to AC-IW-31 still pass" cannot hold, because those criteria include the card and the mark.)

- **AC-IW-60** (auto) [IW-34, R1]: GIVEN the card disabled by configuration, WHEN `inspect` is pressed, THEN it stays bound and dormant, no card opens, and no other instrument behaviour changes.

### G. Evidence, lie tests and playtest

- **AC-IW-48** (manual, retained in `production/qa/evidence/`) [coding standards]: GIVEN a launched build, WHEN captured, THEN the folder holds: the wall dial at every `k` on 1080p (1-5) and on 2160p (1-8); DIAL_S at `k` 1; the card at `H` 3 and `H` 6 on 1080p, at default `H` on 1440p and 2160p, and the 720p `H` 6 capped card (360 px); the Steam Deck case (1280x800, `H` 3 over `k` 2); a wrapped-label card; a LINEAR card at `H` 3 and `H` 6 on 1080p; the 720p `H` 6 fallback fixture of D.6; a lying dial with the card open (wall and card side by side) and a greyscale capture of that scene; an unlit gauge and the housing pip in the dark; the DEAD frame; a LINEAR sight-glass at three levels; the alarm mark on a DIAL_L housing; the notch pair under reduced motion; and the fixture, Chemlight and beam lighting comparison.
- **AC-IW-49** (manual) [HR-5] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*: GIVEN a fixed protocol of 10 gauges per run (5 lying and 5 honest, in random order, no card, no witness; **at least 3 of the 5 honest gauges are in motion** during the observation, one stepping, one mid-settle or sweeping and one trembling under a real stress predicate, the others at rest; the tester is told only that "some gauges lie"), WHEN run three times (Run 1 greyscale and muted: motion mode only; Run 2 muted: gleam plus motion; Run 3 greyscale: motion plus sound), THEN in each run at least 8 of 10 are classified correctly within 20 s each, at most 1 of the 2 resting honest gauges and at most 1 of the 3 moving honest gauges are called lying, and the miss rates are reported per class (at rest, stepping, settling or sweeping, trembling) so that a pass proves more than 'moving means lying'. The thresholds are the defaults applied 2026-10-27, pending user confirmation (D95) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*. The gleam alone cannot be isolated by a player (motion is always present) and is covered by the pixel-hue check of AC-IW-78.
- **AC-IW-50** (manual) [HR-5]: the audio-removed leg of AC-IW-49: GIVEN a lying gauge with the audio muted, WHEN viewed in colour, THEN a tester identifies it by the gleam and the motion mode within 20 s in 8 of 10 trials (this is Run 2 of AC-IW-49, kept as its own id so the HR-5 evidence names each mode pairing). Thresholds and sample sizes: default applied 2026-10-27, pending user confirmation (D95) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*.
- **AC-IW-120** (manual, measured) [IW-27, HR-5, P4 'at least one channel of truth'] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*: GIVEN a greyscale capture with a deuteranopia simulation and muted audio, WHEN testers classify the AC-IW-49 protocol (10 gauges, 5 lying, at least 3 honest gauges in motion), THEN at least 8 of 10 are classified correctly within 20 s each (the motion-mode tell alone, Run 1); and GIVEN the greyscale capture of a lying dial, THEN the gleam fringe holds a luminance contrast of at least `GLEAM_LUM_CONTRAST_MIN` 3:1 against the glass it rides (measured at S-A1). Thresholds and sample sizes: default applied 2026-10-27, pending user confirmation (D95) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*.
- **AC-IW-52** (manual, playtest) `(thresholds: default applied 2026-10-27, pending user confirmation — D95)` [Player Fantasy "We will know this was right if"]: GIVEN at least 6 unprompted first-session players in the MVP kill / continue playtest, WHEN observed, THEN at least 4 of 6 each: confirm a doubted reading against a witness or a second instrument at least once per session; describe a glide as "wandering", "drifting" or "won't settle" and a tremor as "shaking"; do not ask for a ship map or status screen after the first session; and use the card positively: at least 4 of 6 open the card at least once on an instrument of the IW-22 decision table before acting on it (the `card_opened` log with the action that follows within 15 s), while no player opens it on more than 50 % of the instruments they pass lit (opens divided by lit, in-reach instruments; the thresholds are the defaults applied 2026-10-27, pending user confirmation, D95) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*.

### H. Engine, performance and spikes

- **AC-IW-53** (auto) [UI-4, 4.6 dual focus]: GIVEN every instrument and card `Control`, WHEN enumerated recursively, THEN `mouse_filter` is IGNORE and `focus_mode` is NONE in `gameplay` and in `inspect_card`; hovering the card changes nothing.
- **AC-IW-54** (spike) [IW-10, OQ-IW-7]: GIVEN `Sprite2D` rotation versus pre-rotated frames under NEAREST at `k` 1 to 8 for needles of 18 and 24 px, WHEN swept from -120 to +120 degrees in 0.5-degree steps, THEN the rendered needle is one 8-connected pixel run from pivot to tip with no gap and a perpendicular thickness of 2 art px (a measured 3 on diagonals fails unless the ADR accepts it); otherwise the recorded fallback (a pre-rendered needle strip) applies.
- **AC-IW-55** (spike, **BLOCKED until the technical-director accepts or replaces the OQ-IW-10 number**) [OQ-IW-10, IW-12, IW-16] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*: GIVEN 20 on-screen instruments with 4 lying, WHEN the presenter pass runs for 600 frames on the CI runner (the reference machine X14 stays open and is not named; the runner description is recorded in the output), THEN the median and 99th-percentile frame cost are recorded and pass iff the median is at most 0.25 ms and the p99 at most 0.5 ms (the proposal, half of the Filter's `CPU_BUDGET_MS` 0.5 median; the technical-director may replace it and the criterion then reads against that number); the per-frame work is O(1) per instrument (D.1, D.4, D.5, D.11), the predicate subject lists are precomputed at section load (IW-12), and `perceived()` calls equal the on-screen instrument count (AC-IW-62); instrument draw calls are at most 2 per on-screen instrument (face and needle) plus 1 per lying instrument when the gleam fallback is active; the ShaderMaterial count added by instruments is 0 on the per-instance path and 0 on the fallback path, never above the LO-13 five.
- **AC-IW-56** (spike) [IW-14, OQ-IW-14]: GIVEN the bitmap instrument face, WHEN rendered at integer `H`, THEN all digits 0-9 advance equally (tabular), the zero is slashed, and `1`, `l` and `I` are pixel-distinct (ART 7.1 asset test); the locale list of AC-IW-88 either has a face or ships no card.

### I. Cross-system and process

- **AC-IW-105** (lint, routed to the accessibility-specialist next to OQ-IW-13) [IW-20, P4 "at least one channel of truth"]: GIVEN every lie-eligible LINEAR instrument in the manifest and its placement, WHEN linted, THEN a hue-independent TRUTH witness (the pipe flow pattern or the puddle) lies within `WITNESS_HOPS_MAX` of it, so the colour-blind Power-vs-Fuel backup is not left with the lying glass alone; a LINEAR instrument without one is TRUTH-default.
- **AC-IW-106** (review, plus auto at the producer's cross-check) [IW-12, IW-36, P2]: GIVEN each stress source in the IW-12 predicate (i)-(iii), WHEN checked against Adaptive Audio and Failure & Trail, THEN each has a sound tell or a world-witness tell owned there, and in a capture at `k` 1 with the instrument off-screen or its tremor sub-perceptible the tell still reaches the player, so zooming out never removes the 10-30 s warning; a source with only an instrument tremor fails.
- **AC-IW-108** (lint, plus review) [IW-29, OQ-SN-11, SN D.12] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review; BLOCKING 1)*: GIVEN a **test-owned fixture manifest** (a data file under `tests/`, not the indicative table) whose rows carry `comfort_credit` and `alarm_credit` in {FIXED, SCANNER, SUIT_IN_ROOM, NONE} and a lint input `seat_scanner_usable` in {true, false}, WHEN the floor lints run, THEN (a) a SCANNER-only row counts for `seat_scanner_usable` true and not for false; (b) a FIXED row counts for both; (c) a SUIT_IN_ROOM credit counts only for an ALARM-direction lie, only when `mount_room` equals `subject_room`, and never for a COMFORT-direction lie nor for a REMOTE row; (d) a GLYPH never counts as another instrument's fixed partner; (e) per-site counts are reported as the pair `[count(false), count(true)]` and a site whose upper end is below 3 fails advisory floor (ii) while a site whose lower end is below 3 emits a finding; (f) for the indicative manifest as a regression input the reported ranges are home [3, 9], A [0, 4], B [1, 5], and the lint reports no `AT-FLOOR` tag for any site. The two P0 asks (a second `p_o2` dial in derelict A and in derelict B) are recorded as findings, not as passes. No criterion asserts that the indicative manifest as listed passes floor (i).
- **AC-IW-57** (review) [K-1 to K-18, X-3, X-4]: GIVEN this GDD, WHEN reviewed, THEN each carried conflict has a numbered handling, an owner and a route in X-4, every refresh owed to a neighbour is in X-3, and the neighbour GDDs that name this system (Filter, Interaction, Controller, Boarding, Item DB, Derelict Authoring, Emitter State, Lighting, Graph, Chart Table, HUD, Debug Overlay) have each been re-read against the rows in Interactions I-D after they land.
- **AC-IW-58** (lint) [reflexion log]: GIVEN this GDD, WHEN the stale-token list of X-3 (copied into `tools/ci/stale_tokens.txt`) is grepped, THEN it returns no hit outside a rejection, citation or withdrawal line.
- **AC-IW-61** (review) [K-2]: GIVEN the Pipeline owner, WHEN K-2 is raised, THEN the settle exemption (one triple plus the local scalars `prev_honest_pm`, `rest_pm`, the window step and the dwell candidate, closed form, never persisted, cleared and snapped on restore *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*) is acknowledged in an ADR or the conflict is escalated to the technical-director.

### J. Game Feel, Visual/Audio and UI traceability

One criterion per requirement of those three sections that is not already carried above (the settle window and tremor onset are AC-IW-16 and AC-IW-20; zones are AC-IW-24; the dead frame is AC-IW-42; the forbidden surfaces are AC-IW-1).

- **AC-IW-90** (manual, playtest) [Game Feel "Feel Acceptance Criteria" 1]: GIVEN at least 6 first-session players and no prompting, WHEN asked to describe what they saw after a session with one honest, one tremoring and one lying gauge, THEN at least 4 of 6 describe the honest needle as "settling", "stepping" or "catching up", the lying one as "wandering", "drifting" or "won't settle", and the tremor as "shaking" (shared sample with AC-IW-52). Thresholds and sample sizes: default applied 2026-10-27, pending user confirmation (D95) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*.
- **AC-IW-91** (auto, plus measured) [Game Feel input responsiveness] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*: (auto) GIVEN a frame-stepped run at 60 fps, WHEN `inspect` is pressed on a lit target (primary or secondary slot), THEN the card node is drawn on the next render frame (ceiling 2 frames) for presses at tick 0, tick 4 and tick 8 after focus lands and at tick 4 after a close (the debounce delays the plate, never the press), and every close cause of AC-IW-33 closes it in the same render frame by the latency oracle above; the prompt appears `PROMPT_SHOW_DEBOUNCE_TICKS` (13) ticks after a lit focus and hides in the same frame `lit(ref)` falls *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*. (measured) in a playtest of at least 6 testers with 20 presses each on lit, focused instruments, at least 19 of 20 presses per tester open the card, at least 95 % of opens appear within 2 frames in the input log, and no log shows a dropped press. Thresholds and sample sizes: default applied 2026-10-27, pending user confirmation (D95) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*.
- **AC-IW-92** (manual) [Game Feel animation targets]: GIVEN a settle (a closed breaker, 40 pm or more) and a quantum crossing shown side by side to 6 testers, WHEN asked which is the jump, THEN at least 5 of 6 say the settle and none calls either "rubbery"; the measured settle peak is at 220 ms (13 frames) and the exact 2 % time at 299 ms (18 frames) at the defaults *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*. **Stimulus:** three equal-travel clip pairs, each a settle against a linear ramp of the same travel and duration: (a) a 216 pm step (the smallest with a visible 1-px overshoot on DIAL_L), (b) a 397 pm step (the SCRAM step of D.4), and (c) a single-quantum detent against a settle of the same travel, so the test compares settle shape and not travel size (a 205 pm step against a detent tests travel only: its overshoot is 0.9 px). Thresholds and sample sizes: default applied 2026-10-27, pending user confirmation (D95) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*.
- **AC-IW-93** (manual) [Game Feel "Feel Acceptance Criteria" 4]: GIVEN a 10-minute session with both an honest tremor and a lying gauge, WHEN the testers are asked what each means, THEN no more than 1 of 6 confuses a tremor with a lie or a lie with a tremor. Thresholds and sample sizes: default applied 2026-10-27, pending user confirmation (D95) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*.
- **AC-IW-94** (auto, plus measured) [IW-23, Game Feel] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*: (auto) GIVEN a card open under camera drift of up to the look-ahead distance, WHEN each frame is drawn, THEN the plate position equals the re-projected anchor on the HUD grid within 0 px, the side is unchanged unless the latched rect fails (a), (b) or (c), and the plate never intersects the instrument footprint or the chest disc. (measured) over a 600-frame capture under drift, no frame moves the plate by more than one HUD grid step relative to the instrument and the plate never intersects the beam corridor when a clean candidate exists.
- **AC-IW-95** (auto, plus AC-IW-4) [Game Feel "Feel Acceptance Criteria" 6] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*: GIVEN a 600-frame capture with `lit(ref)` oscillating across 120 / 150 and the card open at the start, THEN the card is visible for 0 frames after the first false, there is exactly 1 close event and 0 reopen events, and the pool-rim face visibility is changed only by the renderer.
- **AC-IW-96** (auto) [IW-14, HR-13, Game Feel]: GIVEN a breach changing the card numeral faster than the grid, WHEN luminance events are counted over every rolling second [t, t + 1000 ms), THEN there are at most `1000 / HONEST_TICK_MS` per rolling second (2 at 500 ms; fewer when the HUD skips a boundary; lag at most `2 x HONEST_TICK_MS`), each grid boundary is one event however many digits change, and no event is an inverted plate.
- **AC-IW-97** (manual) [IW-27, Game Feel]: GIVEN reduced motion on, WHEN testers see an honest trembling gauge (held notch) and a lying gauge, THEN at least 5 of 6 can tell them apart. Thresholds and sample sizes: default applied 2026-10-27, pending user confirmation (D95) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*.
- **AC-IW-98** (lint, plus review) [VA-11]: GIVEN the asset manifest, WHEN checked, THEN every P0 asset of VA-11 exists with its named slices and names that follow ART 8.5, sheets are lossless PNG, palette-locked, binary alpha and nearest-filtered, and the state tokens `running`, `stopped`, `whole`, `cracked`, `lit`, `unlit`, `worn` and `dead` are present in the sidecar vocabulary (BR-5).
- **AC-IW-99** (auto) [VA-11 delta 7, IW-26]: GIVEN the card dial export and the world dial export of each size, WHEN diffed, THEN the geometry (band positions, needle length, pivot) differs by zero pixels.
- **AC-IW-100** (spike) [VA-12]: GIVEN the pixel mock S-A1, the card mock S-A2, the needle rasterisation S-A3 and the gleam compositing S-A4, WHEN run, THEN each result is recorded in `production/qa/evidence/` as pass or the named fallback (octagonal arc, DIAL_S bezel cut, outward alarm tick, pre-rendered needle strip, cut order) is adopted; S-A1 also records the Void Steel-on-Alarm-Red contrast ratio and the 25 % greyscale distinguishability of the three patterns.
- **AC-IW-101** (auto) [VA-8, VA-10]: GIVEN each event E1 to E22, WHEN run against an audio-request recorder, THEN honest settle, tremor and digit ticks request no sound; a lying instrument's sound request comes only from the Filter (`FA_LIE_SIG_GAUGE`, `FA_LIE_SIG_SPRITE`) and starts no later than the glide; `card_opened` and `card_closed` are published exactly once per transition; the alarm mark and the DEAD frame request none.
- **AC-IW-102** (lint) [VA-9, PF-4]: GIVEN the scene, WHEN each emitted node is classified, THEN the only nodes bound to a `PerceptionRef` are those with a `lie_surface_id` in the VA-9 corruptible rows; anchor and TRUTH nodes (housing, bands, ticks, alarm mark, DEAD frame, notch, card frame, label) carry no ref and no tag.
- **AC-IW-103** (auto) [UI-4, OQ-IW-21 closed 2026-10-27 by HS-52 and PC-16]: GIVEN the `inspect_card` context, WHEN the card is open, THEN the OS cursor is hidden or inert (no click, hover or focus handling on any card node), so that with `set_aim` live on a mouse there is no second pointer over the beam.
- **AC-IW-104** (review) [UI-6]: GIVEN the pre-production gate, WHEN checked, THEN `design/ux/instruments.md` exists with a section for each UI-6 row before epics are written, and stories that touch the instrument family or the card cite it rather than this GDD.
- **AC-IW-121** (auto) [D.5] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*: GIVEN the stress predicate true for 100 ms (6 ticks) and then false, with `TREMOR_ONSET_MS` 150 and `TREMOR_DECAY_MS` 600, WHEN `env` is sampled every frame, THEN `env` rises to 100 / 150 = 0.667, never exceeds 0.667, does not jump to 1.0 at the predicate's end (`env_off` is latched), decays as 0.667 x (1 - (t - t_off) / 600 ms), reaching 0 at 600 ms after the end, and the drawn index `idx_base + o` stays within [0, `N_idx` - 1] at both end stops; GIVEN `o` non-zero, THEN the drawn tip index differs from `idx_base` by exactly `o` (frame-index space, no rounding collision at 45 degrees).
- **AC-IW-122** (auto) [D.4, IW-11] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*: GIVEN a `p_o2` dial vented from rest at 35 dkPa/s (about 47 pm in 400 ms, above the 40 pm threshold) and a `P` dial opened to vacuum, WHEN sampled, THEN no settle starts (a ramp), because `settle_eligible` is false for both; GIVEN the `load` dial and a 205 pm step from rest, THEN a settle starts with a 9.4 pm overshoot (0.9 px); GIVEN a 397 pm step, THEN the overshoot is 18 pm (1.8 px); GIVEN a 100 pm step, THEN a settle starts and the overshoot is under 1 px (an ease-out).
- **AC-IW-123** (auto) [IW-24] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*: GIVEN a card open, WHEN `lit(ref)` falls, THEN the distinct unlatch request (`UI_INSTRUMENT_CARD_UNLATCH`, requested name) is issued exactly once in the same render frame, with no fade; WHEN the card is closed by `inspect`, `cancel`, B, movement, a context change or a binding loss, THEN no unlatch request is issued; GIVEN the first light-loss close of a profile, THEN the next `lit(ref)` rise shows the `verb_inspect` plate without waiting `PROMPT_SHOW_DEBOUNCE_TICKS`, once, and a second light-loss close does not skip the debounce.
- **AC-IW-124** (lint) [IW-21, IW-31, OQ-IW-39] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*: GIVEN a placement set, WHEN the lint runs with OQ-IW-39 open, THEN `PANEL_GAUGE_VERB_CONFLICT` is an ERROR for any INSTRUMENT on a MACHINE_PANEL that also carries an interactable verb target, the two battery dials of the indicative manifest pass only as `WALL_FACE` beside their panels, and with OQ-IW-39 closed in favour of the secondary slot the same case is a warning and AC-IW-113 asserts the inspect row is anchored to the secondary gauge.
- **AC-IW-125** (auto) [IW-27, D.12] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*: GIVEN every lying manifest instrument and every combination of reduced motion (scale 0.6), sound off or on, captions on or off, and greyscale (a luminance-only render) or colour, WHEN the surviving tells are counted (never-resting needle, luminance gleam above `GLEAM_LUM_CONTRAST_MIN`, sound heard), THEN at least two survive in every combination, the numeral tick is not counted as a separate tell, and the D.12 lint passes at scale 1 against `T_VIS_MAX_S` and at scale 0.6 against `T_VIS_MAX_RM_S` for all seven quantity rows (`P` 4.39 s).
- **AC-IW-126** (auto) [D.3, IW-11 (a)] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*: GIVEN `p_o2` 187 dkPa, `T` -22400 mC and `stock` 620 permille as native `displayed_value`, WHEN the card numeral is formatted, THEN it is built from the native value ('18.7', '-22', '62') and not from the lossy pm triple (a test that rebuilds the digit from `prev_honest_pm` must fail the equality on at least one of the three); GIVEN a one-quantum reversal held by the needle's dwell, THEN the numeral may lead the needle by at most the dwell plus one `HONEST_TICK_MS` boundary (900 ms; 1.4 s with a skipped boundary) and the two differ by at most one quantum.
- **AC-IW-127** (auto) [IW-29, Beat C fixtures] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*: GIVEN the test-owned fixture manifest with `seat_scanner_usable` true and then false, WHEN fixtures A and B are evaluated for a COMFORT-direction lie, THEN with true both are eligible (scanner credit), with false both are reported scanner-dependent and ineligible, a REMOTE row never receives a suit credit in either direction, and adding a FIXED partner to fixture B makes it eligible with false.

### Gaps / untestable as written

1. **AC-IW-55 is BLOCKED, not passing.** The presenter CPU budget has no owner number (OQ-IW-10); until the technical-director sets one the criterion can be run and recorded but not passed or failed.
2. **AC-IW-70's emission leg is BLOCKED** until the Lighting spike sets `PIP_EMIT_MAX` (ART 8.12); the presence / absence leg is testable now.
3. **The pip's size is not asserted** (OQ-IW-12, 4x2 versus 2x2): AC-IW-6 asserts only that the pip exists, is dark-readable as a find-aid and does not light the face.
4. **AC-IW-75 no longer branches** (OQ-IW-13): the D96 default is applied (the card shows the honest tremor; with `reduced_motion` it is suppressed and the held notch shows), pending user confirmation; the old "notch in all modes" branch is removed *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*.
5. **AC-IW-39 is ratified** (user ruling D94, OQ-IW-17) and no longer provisional on the Controller's context-matrix amendment *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*; AC-IW-103 is no longer provisional (K-14 and OQ-IW-21 closed by PC-16 and HS-52) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*.
6. **Thresholds of AC-IW-49 to AC-IW-52, AC-IW-90, AC-IW-92, AC-IW-93 and AC-IW-97** (6 testers, 4-of-6, 5-of-6, 8-of-10, 20 s) are the defaults applied 2026-10-27, pending user confirmation (D95); the draft criteria gave none and a criterion without a sample cannot be passed *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*.
7. **The gleam cannot be tested in isolation by a player** (motion is always present); its hue is covered only by the pixel check (AC-IW-78) and the Filter's spike (AC-IW-27, OQ-IW-8). Whether it survives coloured light is unknown until S-A4.
8. **"No bar, no rating, no schematic" cannot be fully automated** (AC-IW-1): the lint finds names and scripts, and the retained-screenshot sweep is the review leg.
9. **AC-IW-51's guarantee at the floor glide rate** rests on the Filter's wander, not the slew (0.5 px/s, one pixel per 2 s), so it is `(provisional — PF D.4)` and moves if the Filter changes `WANDER_AMP_PX`.
10. **AC-IW-84 waits on Accessibility Settings (undesigned)**: it is marked provisional and is re-checked when that GDD lands (AC-IW-57).
11. **Floor (i) of IW-29 deliberately has no passing assertion for the indicative manifest** (every row marked scanner-dependent has no fixed partner) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*: AC-IW-41, AC-IW-83, AC-IW-108 and AC-IW-127 test the lint against a test-owned fixture manifest, not the content, until Derelict Authoring adds the two asked `p_o2` dials.
12. **Feel criteria are subjective by nature** (AC-IW-90 to AC-IW-97): they are stated as samples with thresholds and as measurable timings where a timing exists (frames, ms), and are not a substitute for the automated criteria above.
13. **The qa-lead pass did not run in the original authoring review** and is still owed on the revised criteria, including the 2026-10-27 re-review rewrites (AC-IW-16, -17, -22, -33, -39, -40, -51, -62, -81, -89, -91, -92, -108, -109, -114 and the new AC-IW-121 to AC-IW-127), which were metric-checked by the revising author only *(Amended 2026-10-27 — re-review)*; the 2026-10-27 rewrites (AC-IW-5, -6, -9, -24, -49, -55, -70, -81, -91, -94, -95 and the new AC-IW-109 to AC-IW-120) were metric-checked by the revising author only, and a qa-lead pass is owed *(added 2026-10-27 — design review)*.
14. **AC-IW-55 stays BLOCKED** until the technical-director accepts or replaces the presenter budget; the proposed number is now stated in the criterion so that it can be run, and the draw-call and material counts are testable without it. **AC-IW-63, -109, -113 and -120 are provisional** on the Graph, Sanity, the Controller and the accessibility tier (OQ-IW-20, -35, -39, -42).

## Open Questions

*Owner is `user` (a decision only the project's owner can take, with a proposed answer so the run is never blocked), `agent` (a named specialist or neighbouring GDD owner) or `GDD` (this document, to settle when a dependency lands). Target is `playtest`, `next GDD`, `ADR` or `spike`. The user-decision items come first. Conflicts K-1 to K-18 are carried in Cross-References X-4 with their owners and routes and are not repeated here; every id the body cites (OQ-IW-1 to OQ-IW-20) is defined below, OQ-IW-21 to OQ-IW-34 were minted in the authoring stages, and OQ-IW-35 to OQ-IW-42 were minted by the 2026-10-27 design review. Decisions already taken in this run and logged (the card ships live at MVP, R1; the closed-form settle exemption, R10; the pip, R21; card-only numerals, C1; `CARD_MAG` 1, C7) are not reopened here.*

### Process

Re-checked against HUD & Suit Layer and Debug Overlay (both Designed) on 2026-10-27; re-check against Accessibility Settings when it is designed, and against the neighbours' own Instruments rows (Interactions I-D) after their refreshes (X-3). This GDD owes `design/ux/instruments.md` before epics (UI-6), consuming the HUD's `design/ux/hud.md` components, a registry merge from its final text (X-5; `registry-candidates.yaml`), ADRs for the nine candidates of X-6, and `/design-review` and `/consistency-check` passes, the latter with a confirming run (the stale-token list of X-3 over this file and its `referenced_by` documents).
